([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/coefficient-of-variation-estimation--39931a706c2e8dfe))
- **coefficient-of-variation-estimation**: Use when replicate (blind duplicate) measurements are available for NMR metabolic biomarker data and you need to quantify and compare measurement reproducibility (CV%, R2) before and after a quality control or technical-variation-removal procedure. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/coefficient-of-variation-estimation--dff39758e3e501ad))
- **coefficient-of-variation-inflation-quantification**: Use when a candidate batch-correction output on multi-batch metabolomics data must be checked for variance inflation: compute per-feature VCs before and after normalization and the fraction of features whose VC increased by &gt;=5% (allowed_vc_increase). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/coefficient-of-variation-inflation-quantification))
- **coefficient-of-variation-precision-computation**: Use when benchmarking transcript quantification tools on spike-in DRS data with an equal-concentration mix (e.g. SIRV E0 from SRR6058583), where lower variability in measured isoform abundances indicates better quantification precision. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/coefficient-of-variation-precision-computation))
- **coefficient-of-variation-qc-assessment**: Use when a multi-batch metabolomics feature-count matrix has been normalized (e.g. by RALPS) and you need QC metrics — batch-wise VC and replicate cross-correlation — to judge whether batch effects were reduced. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/coefficient-of-variation-qc-assessment))
- **coefficient-of-variation-ratio-filtering-samples-vs-qc**: Use when a feature abundance table from MS-DIAL (with a sample annotation file labeling QC vs biological samples) needs quality filtering based on the ratio of coefficient of variation across samples vs QCs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/coefficient-of-variation-ratio-filtering-samples-vs-qc))
- **coefficient-of-variation-threshold-analysis**: Use when you have per-feature CV values from quality control analysis of NMR or MS metabolomic data and need to: (1) establish whether your dataset meets FDA reproducibility standards for downstream biomarker discovery or quantification; (2) benchmark data quality against regulatory thresholds; ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/coefficient-of-variation-threshold-analysis) · 2 copies)
- **coefficient-of-variation-thresholding**: Use when a metabonomic feature matrix is accompanied by a per-feature CV table and non-reproducible spectral features must be removed before downstream MWAS analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/coefficient-of-variation-thresholding))
- **coefficient-of-variation-variance-stabilization-analysis**: Use when evaluating whether an imputation method reduces gene-expression variability across cells of the same type — i.e., you have unimputed and imputed count/log matrices plus cell-type annotations and need a variance-stabilization comparison. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/coefficient-of-variation-variance-stabilization-analysis))
- **coefficient-table-interpretation**: Use when you have per-donor flow cytometry cell proportion data plus donor metadata (age, sex) and need to determine which individual-level factor is most strongly associated with each immune cell population's composition. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/coefficient-table-interpretation))
- **coeluted-peak-deconvolution**: Use when processing LC-MS/MS MRM chromatograms (mzML or vendor-converted raw data) where targeted compounds have coeluting isomers or neighbors on one side of the peak, and accurate per-isomer quantitation requires splitting those overlapping peaks before regression or abundance normalization. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/coeluted-peak-deconvolution))
- **coeluted-peak-fitting**: Use when an MRM ion chromatogram contains overlapping/coeluted peaks for a targeted compound (e.g., isomers sharing a transition, like the PC 34:3 isomers at RTs 4.72, 4.84, and 4.92 min) and per-peak abundances must be separated. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/coeluted-peak-fitting))
- **coeluting-compound-resolution**: Use when analyzing complex GC-MS mixtures where two or more chemical compounds elute at similar or identical retention times, producing overlapping or merged peaks in the raw chromatogram. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/coeluting-compound-resolution) · 4 copies)
- **coeluting-ion-correlation-grouping**: Use when you have IDSL.IPA-generated 'peaklists' and 'peak_alignment' directories plus MS1-level HRMS data and need to group co-eluting MS1 ions (isotopologues, adducts, in-source fragments) into composite spectra clusters for chemical annotation without MS2 data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/coeluting-ion-correlation-grouping))
- **coeluting-ion-separation**: Use when raw GC-MS data (netCDF or mzML format) exhibits overlapping chromatographic peaks—i.e., when multiple analytes elute at similar retention times and produce composite mass spectra with ambiguous m/z signals. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/coeluting-ion-separation) · 2 copies)
- **coeluting-isobaric-lipid-feature-analysis**: Use when an LC-MS/MS lipidomics peak table contains chromatographic features with multiple isobaric coeluting lipid spectral matches and you must quantify feature purity before assigning a confident identification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/coeluting-isobaric-lipid-feature-analysis))
- **coenzyme-a-fractional-abundance-simulation**: Use when benchmarking isotope-correction methods (ElemCor MDT/ULS, original ULS, NRE, FAM) on simulated or measured FAM for large sulfur- or nitrogen-containing metabolites at high nominal instrument resolution, or when validating 34S/13C correction accuracy for CoA-pathway metabolites. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/coenzyme-a-fractional-abundance-simulation))
